#NEA Economy System
calculate_public_revenue = {
	#Customs Duty
	set_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 6999 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 7167 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 11913 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 11865 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 10757 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 1612 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 7097 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 9999 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 1628 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 1099 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	divide_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 10 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 714000 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = cut_in_yangtze_customs_duty_flag }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 0.5 }
		modify_global_flag = {
			flag = cut_in_yangtze_customs_duty_flag
			value = -1
		}
	}
	divide_variable = { ECO_yangtze_customs_duty = 1000000 }
	set_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 10068 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 3900 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 1200 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 1104 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 4205 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 10000 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 9974 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 4099 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 4169 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 4196 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 7071 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 7191 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	divide_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 12 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 714000 }
	divide_variable = { ECO_east_coast_customs_duty = 1000000 }
	set_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 1047 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 3 }	#Three in one.
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 9938 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 7108 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 7152 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 11941 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 4152 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 7137 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 1070 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 4023 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 4121 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 9978 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	divide_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 13 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 714000 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = cut_in_liangguang_customs_duty_flag }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 0.5 }
		modify_global_flag = {
			flag = cut_in_liangguang_customs_duty_flag
			value = -1
		}
	}
	divide_variable = { ECO_liangguang_customs_duty = 1000000 }
	set_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 7832 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 10433 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 3944 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 944 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 6981 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 1150 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 11771 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 3929 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 11764 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_province = 6837 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1 }
	}
	divide_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 10 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 714000 }
	divide_variable = { ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty = 1000000 }
	set_variable = { ECO_customs_duty = ECO_yangtze_customs_duty }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_customs_duty = ECO_east_coast_customs_duty }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_customs_duty = ECO_liangguang_customs_duty }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_customs_duty = ECO_northeast_china_customs_duty }
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_customs_duty = 0.5 }
	}
	#Salt Tax
	set_variable = { ECO_salt_tax = max_manpower_k }
	divide_variable = { ECO_salt_tax = 1000000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_salt_tax = 6.5 }
	every_controlled_state = {
		set_variable = { ECO_salt_tax = state_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_salt_tax = 6.5 }
		divide_variable = { ECO_salt_tax = 1000000 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = promote_agricultural_land_tax_reduction_fin_flag }
		}
		multiply_variable = { ECO_salt_tax = 0.9 }
		every_controlled_state = {
			multiply_variable = { ECO_salt_tax = 0.9 }
		}
	}
	#Consolidated Tax
	set_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = 0 }
	every_controlled_state = {
		set_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = arms_factory_level }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = industrial_complex_level }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = building_level@dockyard }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = state_population_k }
		set_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = infrastructure_level }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_railway_level = {
					state = THIS
					level = 5
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = 5 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_railway_level = {
					state = THIS
					level = 4
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = 4 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_railway_level = {
					state = THIS
					level = 3
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = 3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_railway_level = {
					state = THIS
					level = 2
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_railway_level = {
					state = THIS
					level = 1
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = 1 }
		}
		# add_to_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = building_level@rail_way }	#It doesnt work.
		multiply_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax_index = 0.2 }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = ECO_consolidated_tax_index }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = 0.925 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_core_of = PREV }
			}
			multiply_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = 0.02 }
		}
		divide_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = 1000000 }
		add_to_variable = { PREV.ECO_consolidated_tax = ECO_consolidated_tax }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = promote_abolition_of_likin_fin_flag }
		}
		multiply_variable = { ECO_consolidated_tax = 0.9 }
	}
	#Income Tax
	set_variable = { ECO_income_tax = 1 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_income_tax = ECO_income_tax_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_decision = CHI_first_stage_of_industrial_relocation
				has_decision = CHI_second_stage_of_industrial_relocation
			}
		}
		multiply_variable = { ECO_income_tax = 0.6 }
	}
	#Public Revenue
	set_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_customs_duty }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_salt_tax}
	add_to_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_consolidated_tax}
	add_to_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_income_tax }
	set_variable = { ECO_public_revenue_index = 1 }
	subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_revenue_index = CHI_bureaucracy_consumer_goods_factor }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_public_revenue_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = promote_reform_of_fabi_fin_flag }
		}
		multiply_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = 0.9 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_decision = CHI_eradication_of_corruption }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = 0.8 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_construction_expenditure_refund }
	set_variable = { ECO_public_revenue_shown = ECO_public_revenue }
}
calculate_public_expenditure = {
	#Military Expenditure
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_infantry = num_battalions_with_type@infantry }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_infantry = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_infantry = 2.753 }
	set_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_infantry }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_old_infantry = num_battalions_with_type@old_infantry }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_old_infantry = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_old_infantry = 1.873 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_old_infantry }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_marine = num_battalions_with_type@marine }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_marine = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_marine = 4.13 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_marine }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_paratrooper = num_battalions_with_type@paratrooper }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_paratrooper = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_paratrooper = 3.579 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_paratrooper }

	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_mountaineers = 3.854 }

	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_cavalry = num_battalions_with_type@cavalry }

	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_mechanized = 16.632 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_mechanized }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_amphibious_mechanized = num_battalions_with_type@amphibious_mechanized }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_amphibious_mechanized = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_amphibious_mechanized = 16.632 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_amphibious_mechanized }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_artillery_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@artillery_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_artillery_brigade = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_artillery_brigade = 3.972 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_artillery_brigade }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_anti_tank_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@anti_tank_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_anti_tank_brigade = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_anti_tank_brigade = 3.972 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_anti_tank_brigade }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_anti_air_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@anti_air_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_anti_air_brigade = 1000 }

	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_mot_anti_air_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@mot_anti_air_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_mot_anti_air_brigade = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_mot_anti_air_brigade = 9.516 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_mot_anti_air_brigade }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_mot_rocket_artillery_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@mot_rocket_artillery_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_mot_rocket_artillery_brigade = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_mot_rocket_artillery_brigade = 9.516 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_mot_rocket_artillery_brigade }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_motorized_rocket_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@motorized_rocket_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_motorized_rocket_brigade = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_motorized_rocket_brigade = 9.516 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_motorized_rocket_brigade }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_armor = num_battalions_with_type@light_armor }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_armor = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_armor = 34.689 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_light_armor }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_sp_artillery_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@light_sp_artillery_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_sp_artillery_brigade = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_sp_artillery_brigade = 34.689 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_light_sp_artillery_brigade }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_tank_destroyer_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@light_tank_destroyer_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_tank_destroyer_brigade = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_tank_destroyer_brigade = 34.689 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_light_tank_destroyer_brigade }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_sp_anti_air_brigade = num_battalions_with_type@light_sp_anti_air_brigade }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_sp_anti_air_brigade = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_light_sp_anti_air_brigade = 34.689 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_light_sp_anti_air_brigade }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_amphibious_light_armor = num_battalions_with_type@amphibious_light_armor }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_amphibious_light_armor = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_amphibious_light_armor = 34.689 }
	add_to_variable = { ECO_army_expenditure = ECO_expenditure_of_amphibious_light_armor }
	set_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_medium_armor = num_battalions_with_type@medium_armor }
	divide_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_medium_armor = 1000 }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_expenditure_of_medium_armor = 69.738 }

	}
	if = {

					size > 1
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_construction_expenditure = 0.271 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_decision = CHI_south_xinjiang_highway_construction }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_construction_expenditure = 0.271 }
	}
	if = {

	if = {
		limit = { has_decision = CHI_expand_hanyang_arsenal }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_construction_expenditure = 0.38 }

	if = {

	#Public Expenditure

	add_to_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = ECO_administration_expenditure }
	set_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure_shown = ECO_public_expenditure }
}
calculate_public_balance = {
	#Reset Deficit Ratios
	set_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_public_expenditure = 0 }
	set_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_military_expenditure = 0 }
	set_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_construction_expenditure = 0 }
	set_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_administration_expenditure = 0 }
	#Foreign Exchange Reserves
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ECO_foreign_exchange_reserves > 0 }
			has_country_flag = ECO_foreign_exchange_reserves_available_flag
		}
		add_to_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_foreign_exchange_reserves }
		set_variable = { ECO_foreign_exchange_reserves = 0 }
	}
	#Public Balance
	set_variable = { ECO_public_balance_shown = ECO_public_revenue_shown }
	subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_balance_shown = ECO_public_expenditure }
	set_variable = { ECO_public_balance = ECO_public_revenue }
	subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_balance = ECO_public_expenditure }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { ECO_public_balance < 0 } }
		}
		add_to_variable = { ECO_foreign_exchange_reserves = ECO_public_balance }
	}
	else = {
		#Spending
		set_variable = { ECO_military_spending = 0 }
		set_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = 0 }
		set_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ECO_equal_allocation_flag }
			set_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_military_expenditure }
			divide_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_public_expenditure }
			multiply_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
			set_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_construction_expenditure }
			divide_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_public_expenditure }
			multiply_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
			set_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
			subtract_from_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_military_spending }
			subtract_from_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_construction_spending }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = ECO_administration_spending
				max = ECO_administration_expenditure
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ECO_military_first_flag }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { ECO_military_expenditure < ECO_public_revenue }
				}
				set_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_military_expenditure }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_military_spending }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = ECO_military_expenditure }
				set_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_construction_expenditure }
				divide_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_public_expenditure }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
				set_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_construction_spending }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ECO_administration_spending
					max = ECO_administration_expenditure
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ECO_construction_first_flag }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { ECO_construction_expenditure < ECO_public_revenue }
				}
				set_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_construction_expenditure }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_construction_spending }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = ECO_construction_expenditure }
				set_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_military_expenditure }
				divide_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_public_expenditure }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
				set_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_military_spending }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ECO_administration_spending
					max = ECO_administration_expenditure
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ECO_administration_first_flag }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { ECO_administration_expenditure < ECO_public_revenue }
				}
				set_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_administration_expenditure }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = ECO_administration_spending }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = ECO_administration_expenditure }
				set_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_military_expenditure }
				divide_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_public_expenditure }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
				set_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_military_spending }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ECO_construction_spending
					max = ECO_construction_expenditure
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_public_revenue }
			}
		}
		#Increase in Money Supply
		set_variable = { ECO_increase_in_money_supply = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { ECO_inflation_rate > 20 }
					has_country_flag = CHI_monetary_system_reform_in_progress_flag
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { ECO_military_spending < ECO_military_expenditure }
					has_country_flag = ECO_money_supply_increase_available_for_military_flag
				}
				add_to_variable = { ECO_increase_in_money_supply = ECO_military_expenditure }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_increase_in_money_supply = ECO_military_spending }
				set_variable = { ECO_military_spending = ECO_military_expenditure }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { ECO_construction_spending < ECO_construction_expenditure }
					has_country_flag = ECO_money_supply_increase_available_for_construction_flag
				}
				add_to_variable = { ECO_increase_in_money_supply = ECO_construction_expenditure }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_increase_in_money_supply = ECO_construction_spending }
				set_variable = { ECO_construction_spending = ECO_construction_expenditure }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { ECO_administration_spending < ECO_administration_expenditure }
					has_country_flag = ECO_money_supply_increase_available_for_administration_flag
				}
				add_to_variable = { ECO_increase_in_money_supply = ECO_administration_expenditure }
				subtract_from_variable = { ECO_increase_in_money_supply = ECO_administration_spending }
				set_variable = { ECO_administration_spending = ECO_administration_expenditure }
			}
			set_variable = { ECO_price_index = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { ECO_price_index = ECO_inflation_rate }
			set_variable = { ECO_inflation_rate = ECO_increase_in_money_supply }
			divide_variable = { ECO_inflation_rate = ECO_public_revenue_shown }
			multiply_variable = { ECO_inflation_rate = 0.01 }
			add_to_variable = { ECO_inflation_rate = 1 }
			multiply_variable = { ECO_inflation_rate = ECO_price_index }
			add_to_variable = { ECO_inflation_rate = -1 }
			set_variable = { CHI_monetary_system_reform_cost = ECO_inflation_rate }
			multiply_variable = { CHI_monetary_system_reform_cost = 100 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = establish_four_bank_joint_office_fin_flag }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_monetary_system_reform_cost = 0.5 }
			}
		}
		#Deficits
		set_variable = { ECO_public_spending = ECO_military_spending }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_public_spending = ECO_construction_spending }
		add_to_variable = { ECO_public_spending = ECO_administration_spending }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { ECO_public_spending < ECO_public_expenditure_shown }
			}
			set_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_public_expenditure = ECO_public_expenditure_shown }
			subtract_from_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_public_expenditure = ECO_public_spending }
			divide_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_public_expenditure = ECO_public_expenditure_shown }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { ECO_military_spending < ECO_military_expenditure }
			}
			set_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_military_expenditure = ECO_military_expenditure }
			subtract_from_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_military_expenditure = ECO_military_spending }
			divide_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_military_expenditure = ECO_military_expenditure }
			add_to_variable = { ECO_number_of_times_military_expenditure_in_deficit = 1 }	#We shall not forget it.
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { ECO_construction_spending < ECO_construction_expenditure }
			}
			set_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_construction_expenditure = ECO_construction_expenditure }
			subtract_from_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_construction_expenditure = ECO_construction_spending }
			divide_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_construction_expenditure = ECO_construction_expenditure }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { ECO_administration_spending < ECO_administration_expenditure }
			}
			set_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_administration_expenditure = ECO_administration_expenditure }
			subtract_from_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_administration_expenditure = ECO_administration_spending }
			divide_variable = { ECO_deficit_ratio_of_administration_expenditure = ECO_administration_expenditure }
		}
	}
	#Update and Initialisation
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = CHI_suzhou_jiaxing_railway_construction }

		multiply_variable = { CHI_suzhou_jiaxing_railway_construction_expenditure = ECO_construction_expenditure_index }

		multiply_variable = { CHI_guangzhou_hankou_railway_construction_expenditure = 0.712 }
		multiply_variable = { CHI_guangzhou_hankou_railway_construction_expenditure = ECO_construction_expenditure_index }

		multiply_variable = { CHI_repair_huayuankou_embankment_expenditure = ECO_construction_expenditure_index }
		add_to_variable = { CHI_repair_huayuankou_embankment_expenditure_spent = CHI_repair_huayuankou_embankment_expenditure }
	}
	set_variable = { ECO_construction_expenditure_refund = 0 }
	set_variable = { ECO_construction_expenditure_added = 0 }

		limit = { NOT = { has_decision = CHI_expand_zhuzhou_arsenal } }
		divide_variable = { CHI_expand_zhuzhou_arsenal_remove_days = ECO_arms_factory_construction_remove_days_index }
		round_variable = CHI_expand_zhuzhou_arsenal_remove_days
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_decision = CHI_expand_gongxian_arsenal } }
		divide_variable = { CHI_expand_gongxian_arsenal_remove_days = ECO_arms_factory_construction_remove_days_index }
		round_variable = CHI_expand_gongxian_arsenal_remove_days
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_decision = CHI_burma_road_construction } }
		divide_variable = { CHI_burma_road_construction_remove_days = ECO_infrastructure_construction_remove_days_index }
		round_variable = CHI_burma_road_construction_remove_days
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_decision = CHI_repair_huayuankou_embankment } }
		divide_variable = { CHI_repair_huayuankou_embankment_remove_days = ECO_infrastructure_construction_remove_days_index }
		round_variable = CHI_repair_huayuankou_embankment_remove_days
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		set_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure_1 = ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure }
		divide_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure_1 = 2 }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure_1 = -1 }
		set_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure_2 = 0 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure_1 = 0 }
		set_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure_2 = ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure_2 = -1 }
	}
	# set_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_army_org_factor = ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure }
	# multiply_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_army_org_factor = -1 }
	# set_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_army_defence_factor = ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure }
	# multiply_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_army_defence_factor = -1 }
	# set_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_breakthrough_factor = ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure }
	# multiply_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_breakthrough_factor = -1 }
	set_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_production_speed_buildings_factor = ECO_deficit_influence_of_construction_expenditure }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_production_speed_buildings_factor = -1 }
	set_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_political_power_factor = ECO_deficit_influence_of_administration_expenditure }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_budget_deficit_political_power_factor = -1 }
	#Influence of Inflation
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ECO_inflation_rate > 0 }
		}
		set_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence = ECO_inflation_rate }
		divide_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence = 20 }
		multiply_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence = ECO_inflation_influence_index }
	}
	set_variable = { ECO_economic_situation_stability_factor = ECO_inflation_influence }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_economic_situation_stability_factor = -1 }
	set_variable = { ECO_economic_situation_war_support_factor = ECO_inflation_influence }
	multiply_variable = { ECO_economic_situation_war_support_factor = -1 }
	#Budget Deficit
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_budget_deficit_dynamic_modifier } }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure > 0 }
				check_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_construction_expenditure > 0 }
				check_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_administration_expenditure > 0 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_budget_deficit_dynamic_modifier }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_budget_deficit_dynamic_modifier }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_military_expenditure > 0 }
				check_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_construction_expenditure > 0 }
				check_variable = { ECO_deficit_influence_of_administration_expenditure > 0 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_budget_deficit_dynamic_modifier }
		}
	}
	#Economic Situation
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_1 }
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			}
			check_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence > 0 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			check_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence > 0.33 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			check_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence > 0.66 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_3 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence > 0.66 } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence > 0.33 } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_1 }
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			}
			NOT = { check_variable = { ECO_inflation_influence > 0 } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_1 } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_3 } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ECO_economic_situation_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			}
		}
	}
}
update_public_revenue_and_expenditure = {
	CHI = {
		calculate_public_revenue = yes
		calculate_public_expenditure = yes
		every_subject_country = {
			limit = { is_literally_china = yes }
			calculate_public_revenue = yes
			calculate_public_expenditure = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_separative_clique }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = 0.25 }
				add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_public_revenue = ECO_public_revenue }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = 3 }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = 0.25 }
				add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_public_expenditure = ECO_public_expenditure }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_obedient_clique }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = 0.5 }
				add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_public_revenue = ECO_public_revenue }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = 0.5 }
				add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_public_expenditure = ECO_public_expenditure }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_incorporated_clique }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = 0.75 }
				add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_public_revenue = ECO_public_revenue }
				divide_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = 3 }
				multiply_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = 0.75 }
				add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_public_expenditure = ECO_public_expenditure }
				divide_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {	#Just in case.
				limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet }
				add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_public_revenue = ECO_public_revenue }
				set_variable = { ECO_public_revenue = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { CHI.ECO_public_expenditure = ECO_public_expenditure }
				set_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure = 0 }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { ECO_public_revenue_shown = ECO_public_revenue }
		set_variable = { ECO_public_expenditure_shown = ECO_public_expenditure }
	}
}

#China
swap_industrialisation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_industrialisation_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 3

swap_or_remove_bureaucracy = {
	if = {
		limit = { need_to_remove_bureaucracy = yes }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_1 } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_2 } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_3 } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = remove_bureaucracy_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			need_to_remove_bureaucracy = no
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_political_power_gain > -0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_mobilization_laws_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_trade_laws_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_economy_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_political_advisor_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_tank_manufacturer_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_naval_manufacturer_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_aircraft_manufacturer_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_materiel_manufacturer_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_industrial_concern_cost_factor < 0.01 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_2 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = swap_bureaucracy_1_to_2_intro
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			need_to_remove_bureaucracy = no
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_stability_factor > -0.11 }
			check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_consumer_goods_factor < 0.01 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_3 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = swap_bureaucracy_1_to_3_intro
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_political_power_gain > -0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_mobilization_laws_cost_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_trade_laws_cost_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_economy_cost_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_political_advisor_cost_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_tank_manufacturer_cost_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_naval_manufacturer_cost_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_aircraft_manufacturer_cost_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_materiel_manufacturer_cost_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_industrial_concern_cost_factor < 0.01 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_1 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = swap_bureaucracy_2_to_1_intro
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_stability_factor > -0.11 }
				check_variable = { CHI_bureaucracy_consumer_goods_factor < 0.01 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_bureaucracy_dynamic_modifier_1 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = swap_bureaucracy_3_to_1_intro
	}
}
swap_national_quality = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_war_support_factor > -0.06 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_training_time_army_factor < 0.31 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_mobilization_speed > -0.21 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_local_intel_to_enemies < 0.06 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_drift_defence_factor > -0.21 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_research_speed_factor > -0.36 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_1 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_2 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = swap_national_quality_1_to_2_intro
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_war_support_factor > 0 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_conscription_factor > -0.11 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_training_time_army_factor < 0.01 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_mobilization_speed > 0 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_local_intel_to_enemies < 0 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_drift_defence_factor > 0 }
			check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_research_speed_factor > -0.21 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_3 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = swap_national_quality_2_to_3_intro
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_war_support_factor > 0 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_conscription_factor > -0.11 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_training_time_army_factor < 0.01 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_mobilization_speed > 0 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_local_intel_to_enemies < 0 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_drift_defence_factor > 0 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_research_speed_factor > -0.21 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_3 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_2 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = swap_national_quality_3_to_2_intro
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_war_support_factor > -0.06 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_training_time_army_factor < 0.31 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_mobilization_speed > -0.21 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_local_intel_to_enemies < 0.06 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_drift_defence_factor > -0.21 }
				check_variable = { CHI_national_quality_research_speed_factor > -0.36 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_2 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_national_quality_dynamic_modifier_1 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = swap_national_quality_2_to_1_intro
	}
}
set_pitched_battle = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = CHI_set_pitched_battle_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = CHI_pitched_battle
			scope = CHI
		}
	}
}
clean_pitched_battle = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = CHI_clean_pitched_battle_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_pitched_battle }
	}
}
set_counter_offensive_operation = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = CHI_counter_offensive_operation_states
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = CHI_counter_offensive_operation
			scope = CHI
		}
	}
}
clean_counter_offensive_operation = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = CHI_counter_offensive_operation_states
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CHI_counter_offensive_operation }
	}
	clear_array = CHI_counter_offensive_operation_states
}
#Military Aid
calculate_freight_volume_and_expenditure = {
	#Check the Ocean Transport
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_global_flag = pacific_war_start_flag }
					NOT = {
						any_state = {
							OR = {
								state = 592
								state = 620
							}
							is_controlled_by_enemy_or_ally = yes
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_global_flag = pacific_war_start_flag
					NOT = {
						any_state = {
							OR = {
								state = 592
								state = 646
							}
							is_controlled_by_enemy_or_ally = yes
						}
					}
					any_state = {
						OR = {
							state = 524
							state = 623
						}
						is_controlled_by_enemy_or_ally = no
					}
				}
			}
			# any_country = {
			# 	OR = {
			# 		original_tag = CHI
			# 		AND = {
			# 			is_literally_china = yes
			# 			is_ally_with = CHI
			# 		}
			# 	}
			# 	naval_base > 0
			# }
		}
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_ocean_transport = 5 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_ocean_transport = 0 }
	}
	#Check the Sino-Burmese transport Line (Burma Road)
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = burma_road_construction_fin_flag
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = RW_burma_road_closed_flag
				has_global_flag = RW_sino_japanese_war_end_flag
			}
			NOT = {
				any_state = {
					OR = {
						state = 288
						state = 325
						state = 640
						state = 747
					}
					is_controlled_by_enemy_or_ally = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_burmese_transport_line = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_burmese_transport_line = 0 }
	}
	#Check the Sino-Indian transport Line (The Hump)
	if = {
		limit = {
			CHI = { has_completed_focus = CHI_the_hump }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = RW_sino_japanese_war_end_flag }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				date > 1943.12.31
				check_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line < 0.5 }
			}
			set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line = 0.5 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				date > 1943.7.31
				check_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line < 0.25 }
			}
			set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line = 0.25 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				date > 1942.12.31
				check_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line < 0.1 }
			}
			set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line = 0.1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line < 0.05 }
			}
			set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line = 0.05 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line = 0 }
	}
	#Check the Sino-Soviet transport Line (Northwest China)
	if = {
		limit = {
			CHI = { has_completed_focus = CHI_purchase_soviet_arms_and_ammunition }
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = RW_soviet_japanese_neutrality_pact_flag
				has_global_flag = RW_sino_japanese_war_end_flag
			}
			NOT = {
				any_state = {
					OR = {
						state = 283
						state = 617
						state = 916
					}
					is_controlled_by_enemy_or_ally = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_soviet_transport_line = 0.25 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = { has_completed_focus = CHI_development_of_northwest_china }
			}
			multiply_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_soviet_transport_line = 1.1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_soviet_transport_line = 0 }
	}
	#Check the Sino-Vietnamese transport Line (Yunnan-Vietnam Railway & Guangxi-Vietnam Road)
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = RW_sino_japanese_war_end_flag }
			671 = { is_controlled_by_enemy_or_ally = no }
			any_state = {
				OR = {
					state = 325
					state = 594
				}
				is_controlled_by_enemy_or_ally = no
			}
		}
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_vietnamese_transport_line = 0.5 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_state = {
						OR = {
							state = 325
							state = 594
						}
						is_controlled_by_enemy_or_ally = yes
					}
				}
			}
			multiply_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_vietnamese_transport_line = 2 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_vietnamese_transport_line = 0 }
	}
	#Calculate the Freight Volume and Expenditure
	set_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_basic = CHI_freight_volume_of_ocean_transport }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_basic = CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_burmese_transport_line }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_freight_volume_basic = CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_vietnamese_transport_line }
	set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume = CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_soviet_transport_line }
	set_variable = { CHI_american_military_aid_freight_volume = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_american_military_aid_freight_volume = CHI_freight_volume_of_sino_indian_transport_line }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = german_military_aid_third_batch_fin_flag }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_freight_volume_needed > CHI_freight_volume_basic } }
			set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
			multiply_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 800 }
			round_variable = CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1
			set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
			multiply_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = 80 }
			round_variable = CHI_german_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1
			set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
			multiply_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure = 1.6 }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_german_military_aid_round = 1 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 1500 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure = 3 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure_spent }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_german_military_aid_round = 2 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = 180 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = CHI_german_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure = 3.6 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure_spent }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 2000 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_german_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure = 4 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_german_military_aid_expenditure_spent }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = foreign_military_aid_second_batch_fin_flag }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_freight_volume_needed > CHI_freight_volume_basic } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_foreign_military_aid_round = 1 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0 = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0 = 773.5 }
				round_variable = CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 145 }
				round_variable = CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1 = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1 = 9.1 }
				round_variable = CHI_foreign_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0 = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0 = 653.1 }
				round_variable = CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 408.2 }
				round_variable = CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1
			}
			set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_freight_volume_basic }
			multiply_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_expenditure = 1.6 }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_foreign_military_aid_round = 1 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0 = 8000 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0 = CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 1500 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1 = 94 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1 = CHI_foreign_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_expenditure = 16.548 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_foreign_military_aid_expenditure_spent }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0 = 4000 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0 = CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_0_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 2500 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_foreign_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_expenditure = 9.8 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_foreign_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_foreign_military_aid_expenditure_spent }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = soviet_military_aid_fourth_batch_fin_flag }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume_needed > CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_soviet_military_aid_round = 1 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = 49.2 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1 = 11.2 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_soviet_military_aid_round = 2 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 117.1 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1 = 68.3 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_soviet_military_aid_round = 3 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 328.2 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = 6.2 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1 = 41 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 91.4 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = 34.3 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
				multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1 = 36.6 }
				round_variable = CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1
			}
			set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_soviet_military_aid_freight_volume }
			multiply_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_expenditure = 1.3 }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_soviet_military_aid_round = 1 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = 360 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_artillery_equipment_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1 = 82 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_light_tank_chassis_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_expenditure = 9.368 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_soviet_military_aid_expenditure_spent }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_soviet_military_aid_round = 2 } }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = 1200 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1 = 700 }
				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1 = CHI_soviet_military_aid_motorized_equipment_1_arrived }
				set_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_expenditure = 13.12 }

			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { CHI_activate_soviet_military_aid_round = 3 } }

				subtract_from_variable = { CHI_soviet_military_aid_expenditure = CHI_soviet_military_aid_expenditure_spent }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {

					round_variable = CHI_american_military_aid_infantry_equipment_1_actual

estimate_influence_of_he_clique = {
	set_variable = { CHI_he_influence = CHI_he_influence_increase }
	every_character = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = chi_he_clique_flag }
		if = {
			limit = { rank_is_general_of_first_rank = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 5 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { rank_is_general_of_second_rank = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 4 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { rank_is_actual_general = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 3 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { rank_is_lieutenant_general = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 2 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { rank_is_major_general = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_political_advisor = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 2 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_theorist = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 2 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_army_chief = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 2 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_navy_chief = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 2 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_air_chief = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 2 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_high_command = yes }
			CHI = { add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence = 2 } }
		}
	}
}

estimate_power_of_every_clique = {
	#Total Influence
	estimate_influence_of_he_clique = yes
	estimate_influence_of_chen_clique = yes
	estimate_influence_of_hu_clique = yes
	estimate_influence_of_tang_clique = yes
	set_variable = { CHI_total_influence = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_total_influence = CHI_he_influence}
	add_to_variable = { CHI_total_influence = CHI_chen_influence }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_total_influence = CHI_hu_influence }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_total_influence = CHI_tang_influence }
	#Power of Every Clique
	# 保留我们在on_actions中计算的CHI_he_power值
	# set_variable = { CHI_he_power = CHI_he_influence }
	# divide_variable = { CHI_he_power = CHI_total_influence }
	set_variable = { CHI_chen_power = CHI_chen_influence }
	divide_variable = { CHI_chen_power = CHI_total_influence }
	set_variable = { CHI_hu_power = CHI_hu_influence }
	divide_variable = { CHI_hu_power = CHI_total_influence }
	set_variable = { CHI_tang_power = CHI_tang_influence }
	divide_variable = { CHI_tang_power = CHI_total_influence }
	#Power of Progressive and Conservative
	set_variable = { CHI_progressive_power = 0 }
	set_variable = { CHI_conservative_power = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = chen_vs_he_hu_tang_flag }
		add_to_variable = { CHI_progressive_power = CHI_chen_influence }
		add_to_variable = { CHI_conservative_power = CHI_he_influence }
		add_to_variable = { CHI_conservative_power = CHI_hu_influence }
		add_to_variable = { CHI_conservative_power = CHI_tang_influence }
	}
	divide_variable = { CHI_progressive_power = CHI_total_influence }
	divide_variable = { CHI_conservative_power = CHI_total_influence }
}
strengthen_power_of_he_clique_by_five = {
	#x = nT^2/[(1-n)T-a]
	#where x is the influence increase, n is the power increase (0~1), T is total influence and a is clique influence.
	set_temp_variable = { temp1 = CHI_total_influence }
	multiply_temp_variable = { temp1 = temp1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { temp1 = 0.05 }
	set_temp_variable = { temp2 = CHI_total_influence }
	multiply_temp_variable = { temp2 = 0.95 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { temp2 = CHI_he_influence }
	divide_temp_variable = { temp1 = temp2 }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_he_influence_increase = temp1 }
	hidden_effect = { estimate_power_of_every_clique = yes }
	custom_effect_tooltip = strengthen_power_of_he_clique_by_five_tt
}
strengthen_power_of_chen_clique_by_five = {
	#x = nT^2/[(1-n)T-a]
	#where x is the influence increase, n is the power increase (0~1), T is total influence and a is clique influence.
	set_temp_variable = { temp1 = CHI_total_influence }
	multiply_temp_variable = { temp1 = temp1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { temp1 = 0.05 }
	set_temp_variable = { temp2 = CHI_total_influence }
	multiply_temp_variable = { temp2 = 0.95 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { temp2 = CHI_chen_influence }
	divide_temp_variable = { temp1 = temp2 }
	add_to_variable = { CHI_chen_influence_increase = temp1 }
	hidden_effect = { estimate_power_of_every_clique = yes }
	custom_effect_tooltip = strengthen_power_of_chen_clique_by_five_tt
}

swap_intervene_in_china = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_1
			CHI = { surrender_progress > 0.14 }
			date > 1937.10.1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_1
			add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_2 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = { surrender_progress > 0.28 }
				date > 1938.1.1
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_2
				add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_3
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = {
					is_ai = no
					surrender_progress < 0.15
				}
				MAN = { surrender_progress > 0.05 }
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_2
				add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_1
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_3 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = { surrender_progress > 0.42 }
				date > 1938.10.1
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_3
				add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_4
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = {
					is_ai = no
					surrender_progress < 0.29
				}
				MAN = { surrender_progress > 0.01 }
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_3
				add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_2
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_4 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = { surrender_progress > 0.56 }
				date > 1940.1.1
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_4
				add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_5
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = {
					is_ai = no
					surrender_progress < 0.43
				}
				date < 1942.1.1
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_4
				add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_3
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_5 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = { surrender_progress > 0.7 }
				date > 1941.8.1
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_5
				add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_6
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				CHI = {
					is_ai = no
					surrender_progress < 0.57
				}
				date < 1944.3.1
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_5
				add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_4
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_6
			CHI = {
				is_ai = no
				surrender_progress < 0.71
			}
			date < 1945.1.1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_6
			add_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_5
		}
	}
}
JAP_intervene_in_china_remove = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_6 }
		remove_ideas = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_6
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_5 }
		remove_ideas = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_5
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_4 }
		remove_ideas = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_4
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_3 }
		remove_ideas = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_2 }
		remove_ideas = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_2
	}
	else = { remove_ideas = idea_JAP_intervene_in_china_1 }
}

swap_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_1
			surrender_progress > 0.1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_1
			add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_2 }
		if = {

				remove_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_2
				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_3

		}
		else_if = {

				remove_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_2
				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_1
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_3 }
		if = {
			limit = { surrender_progress > 0.3 }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_3
				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_4
			}
		}
		else_if = {

			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_3
				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_2
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {

			swap_ideas = {

				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_5
			}
		}
		else_if = {

				remove_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_4
				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_3
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {

		if = {
			limit = { surrender_progress > 0.5 }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_5
				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_6
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { surrender_progress < 0.41 }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_5
				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_4
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_6 }

			swap_ideas = {

				add_idea = idea_CHI_every_inch_of_land_defended_with_blood_7

set_popularity_100_like_root = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_government = neutrality } }
			set_popularities = {
				neutrality = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_government = democratic } }
			set_popularities = {
				democratic = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_government = communism } }
			set_popularities = {
				communism = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_government = fascism } }
			set_popularities = {
				fascism = 100
			}
		}
	}
}
set_popularity_100_like_from = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_government = neutrality } }
			set_popularities = {
				neutrality = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_government = democratic } }
			set_popularities = {
				democratic = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_government = communism } }
			set_popularities = {
				communism = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_government = fascism } }
			set_popularities = {
				fascism = 100
			}
		}
	}
}
set_popularity_100_like_prev = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { has_government = neutrality } }
			set_popularities = {
				neutrality = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { has_government = democratic } }
			set_popularities = {
				democratic = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { has_government = communism } }
			set_popularities = {
				communism = 100
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { has_government = fascism } }
			set_popularities = {
				fascism = 100
			}
		}
	}
}
remove_rank = {	#Mind the order.
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = major_general_1 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = major_general_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = lieutenant_general_1 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = lieutenant_general_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = actual_general_1 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = actual_general_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = general_of_second_rank_1 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = general_of_second_rank_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = general_of_first_rank_1 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = general_of_first_rank_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = major_general_2 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = major_general_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = lieutenant_general_2 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = lieutenant_general_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = actual_general_2 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = actual_general_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = general_of_second_rank_2 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = general_of_second_rank_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_trait = general_of_first_rank_2 }
		remove_unit_leader_trait = general_of_first_rank_2
	}
}
promote_to_lieutenant_general = {
	if = {
		limit = { rank_is_major_general = yes }
#		remove_rank = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = chi_recruitment_before_civil_war_flag }
			add_unit_leader_trait = lieutenant_general_2
		}
		else = { add_unit_leader_trait = lieutenant_general_1 }
#		add_timed_unit_leader_trait = {
#			trait = recently_promoted
#			days = 100
#		}
#		if = {
#			limit = { has_character_flag = less_than_three_years_since_promotion_flag }
#			clr_character_flag = less_than_three_years_since_promotion_flag
#			set_character_flag = less_than_three_years_since_promotion_flag
#		}
#		else = { set_character_flag = less_than_three_years_since_promotion_flag }
	}
}

promote_to_general_of_first_rank = {
	if = {
		limit = { rank_below_general_of_first_rank = yes }
#		remove_rank = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = chi_recruitment_before_civil_war_flag }
			add_unit_leader_trait = general_of_first_rank_2
		}
		else = { add_unit_leader_trait = general_of_first_rank_1 }
#		add_timed_unit_leader_trait = {
#			trait = recently_promoted
#			days = 100
#		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_field_marshal = no }
			promote_leader = yes
		}
	}
}
switch_rank_version = {
	every_character = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = general_of_first_rank_1
				has_trait = general_of_second_rank_1
				has_trait = actual_general_1
				has_trait = lieutenant_general_1
				has_trait = major_general_1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = general_of_first_rank_1 }
			remove_unit_leader_trait = general_of_first_rank_1
			add_unit_leader_trait = general_of_first_rank_2
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = general_of_second_rank_1 }
			remove_unit_leader_trait = general_of_second_rank_1
			demote_leader = yes
			add_unit_leader_trait = general_of_second_rank_2
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = actual_general_1 }
			remove_unit_leader_trait = actual_general_1
			add_unit_leader_trait = actual_general_2
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = lieutenant_general_1 }
			remove_unit_leader_trait = lieutenant_general_1
			add_unit_leader_trait = lieutenant_general_2
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = major_general_1 }
			remove_unit_leader_trait = major_general_1
			add_unit_leader_trait = major_general_2
		}
	}
}
